# Debug Toolpack 11.3.4 — сгенерировано tools/gen3.py. Правьте генератор, а не этот файл.
dtp_remember_class = {
	set_star_flag = dtp_oc_known
}

dtp_star_fix = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_star_flag = dtp_marked }
			NOT = { OR = {  } }
		}
		dtp_remember_class = yes
		remove_star_flag = dtp_gray
		log = "DTP STARFIX restored"
	}
}

dtp_nav_set = {
	save_global_event_target_as = dtp_cur
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_nb_1
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_nb_1
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_nb_2
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_nb_2
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_nb_3
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_nb_3
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_nb_4
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_nb_4
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_nb_5
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_nb_5
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_nb_6
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_nb_6
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_nb_7
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_nb_7
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_nb_8
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_nb_8
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_nb_9
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_nb_9
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_nb_10
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_nb_10
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_nb_11
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_nb_11
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_nb_12
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_nb_12
	}
	set_variable = { which = dtp_nbn value = 0 }
	every_neighbor_system = {
		prev = {
			change_variable = { which = dtp_nbn value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				prev = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_nbn value = 1 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_nb_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				prev = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_nbn value = 2 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_nb_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				prev = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_nbn value = 3 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_nb_3
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				prev = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_nbn value = 4 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_nb_4
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				prev = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_nbn value = 5 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_nb_5
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				prev = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_nbn value = 6 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_nb_6
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				prev = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_nbn value = 7 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_nb_7
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				prev = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_nbn value = 8 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_nb_8
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				prev = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_nbn value = 9 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_nb_9
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				prev = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_nbn value = 10 }

			}

		}
	}
}

dtp_mark_sys = {
	log = "DTP MARK start"
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_star_flag = dtp_marked }
		}
		set_star_flag = dtp_marked
		log = "DTP MARK flag set"
		dtp_flag_add = yes
	}
}

dtp_flag_add = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = dtp_starhide }
			exists = star
			NOT = { any_system_megastructure = { is_megastructure_type = dtp_mark_mega } }
		}
		star = {
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_mm_loc
		}
		spawn_megastructure = { type = dtp_mark_mega name = "dtp_mark_mega_name" planet = event_target:dtp_mm_loc orbit_distance = 0 }
		log = "DTP MARK: red marker placed"
	}
}

dtp_flag_del = {
	every_system_megastructure = {
		limit = {
			is_megastructure_type = dtp_mark_mega
		}
		remove_megastructure = this
	}
}

dtp_beacon_add = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_host
			NOT = { has_global_flag = dtp_nobeacon }
			NOT = { any_fleet_in_system = { has_fleet_flag = dtp_beacon } }
		}
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_bc_sys
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = star
			}
			star = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_bc_sys
			}
		}
		event_target:dtp_host = {
			create_fleet = {
				name = "dtp_beacon_fleet"
				effect = {
					set_owner = event_target:dtp_host
					create_ship = {
						name = "dtp_beacon_ship"
						design = "DTP_Marker"
					}
					set_location = { target = event_target:dtp_bc_sys distance = 6 angle = random }
					set_fleet_flag = dtp_beacon
					set_fleet_stance = passive
					set_cloaking_active = yes
					every_owned_ship = {
						add_modifier = { modifier = dtp_beacon_ship days = -1 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

dtp_beacon_del = {
	every_fleet_in_system = {
		limit = {
			has_fleet_flag = dtp_beacon
		}
		delete_fleet = this
	}
}

dtp_unmark_sys = {
	log = "DTP UNMARK"
	dtp_beacon_del = yes
	dtp_flag_del = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_star_flag = dtp_marked
		}
		remove_star_flag = dtp_marked
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = dtp_graymode
			}
			set_star_flag = dtp_gray
		}
		else = {
			dtp_remember_class = yes
			else = {
				log = "DTP UNMARK: original class unknown"
			}
			remove_star_flag = dtp_gray
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { OR = {  } }
				}
				log = "DTP UNMARK: star still NOT original after set_star_class"
			}
			else = {
				log = "DTP UNMARK: star restored"
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_star_flag = dtp_renamed
			}
			remove_star_flag = dtp_renamed
			star = {
				save_event_target_as = dtp_star
			}
			set_name = event_target:dtp_star
			log = "DTP UNMARK name restored"
		}
	}
}

dtp_gray_on = {
	set_global_flag = dtp_graymode
	every_system = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_star_flag = dtp_marked }
		}
		dtp_remember_class = yes
		set_star_flag = dtp_gray
	}
	log = "DTP GRAY on"
}

dtp_gray_off = {
	remove_global_flag = dtp_graymode
	every_system = {
		limit = {
			has_star_flag = dtp_gray
			NOT = { has_star_flag = dtp_marked }
		}
		remove_star_flag = dtp_gray
	}
	log = "DTP GRAY off"
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_ml_1

	}
	if = {

		clear_global_event_target = dtp_ml_10
	}
	from = {
		set_variable = { which = dtp_mcount value = 0 }
		every_system = {
			limit = {
				has_star_flag = dtp_marked
			}
			prev = {
				change_variable = { which = dtp_mcount value = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					prev = {

					}
				}
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_ml_1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					prev = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_mcount value = 2 }
					}
				}
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_ml_2
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					prev = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_mcount value = 3 }
					}
				}
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_ml_3
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					prev = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_mcount value = 4 }
					}
				}
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_ml_4
			}
			if = {

						check_variable = { which = dtp_mcount value = 5 }

				}
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_ml_5
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					prev = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_mcount value = 6 }
					}
				}
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_ml_6
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					prev = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_mcount value = 7 }
					}
				}
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_ml_7
			}
			if = {

dtp_war_sync = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = { has_modifier = dtp_war_lock AND = { NOT = { has_global_flag = dtp_war_soft } OR = { AND = { OR = { is_ai = no has_global_flag = dtp_war_ai } has_global_flag = dtp_rule_nopvp } has_country_flag = dtp_rule_np } } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = dtp_war_soft } OR = { AND = { OR = { is_ai = no has_global_flag = dtp_war_ai } has_global_flag = dtp_rule_nopvp } has_country_flag = dtp_rule_np }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_modifier = dtp_war_lock }
				}
				add_modifier = { modifier = dtp_war_lock days = -1 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_modifier = dtp_war_lock
		}
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = { has_modifier = dtp_war_shield AND = { NOT = { has_global_flag = dtp_war_soft } has_country_flag = dtp_rule_prot } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = dtp_war_soft } has_country_flag = dtp_rule_prot
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_modifier = dtp_war_shield }
				}
				add_modifier = { modifier = dtp_war_shield days = -1 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_modifier = dtp_war_shield
		}
	}
}

dtp_poi_sync_all = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = no
			OR = { dtp_has_any_perm = yes has_event_chain = dtp_marks_chain }
		}
		dtp_poi_sync = yes
	}
}

dtp_pidx_ensure = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_variable_set = dtp_pidx }
		}
		set_country_flag = dtp_pidx_self
		set_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 1 }
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_variable_set = dtp_pidx
				NOT = { has_country_flag = dtp_pidx_self }
			}
			prev = {
				change_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 1 }
			}
		}
		remove_country_flag = dtp_pidx_self
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value > 16 }
			}
			set_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 16 }
		}
		set_variable = { which = dtp_amount value = 1000 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_val value = 10 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_years value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_res value = 1 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_tab value = 15 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_in value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_cpage value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_cn value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_coff value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_shn value = 1 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_shz value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_amt value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_stock value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_1 value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_2 value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_3 value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_4 value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_5 value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_6 value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_7 value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_8 value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_1 value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_2 value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_3 value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_4 value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_5 value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_6 value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_7 value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_8 value = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_variable_set = dtp_mcount }
		}
		set_variable = { which = dtp_mcount value = 0 }
	}
}

dtp_init_targets = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = event_target:dtp_tc_1 }
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_tc_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = event_target:dtp_rcp_1 }

dtp_load = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = event_target:dtp_tc_1
			}
			event_target:dtp_tc_1 = {
				save_event_target_as = dtp_t
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = event_target:dtp_rcp_1
			}
			event_target:dtp_rcp_1 = {
				save_event_target_as = dtp_r
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = event_target:dtp_tp_1
			}
			event_target:dtp_tp_1 = {
				save_event_target_as = dtp_p
				solar_system = {
					save_event_target_as = dtp_s
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = event_target:dtp_tf_1
			}
			event_target:dtp_tf_1 = {
				save_event_target_as = dtp_f
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_pidx value = 2 }

dtp_log = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { exists = event_target:dtp_log8_actor }
		}
		from = {
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_actor
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_1 value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_1 value = 0 }
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_actor
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_tgt
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_2 value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_2 value = 0 }
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log3_actor
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log3_tgt
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_3 value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_3 value = 0 }
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log4_actor
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log4_tgt
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_4 value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_4 value = 0 }
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log5_actor
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log5_tgt
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_5 value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_5 value = 0 }
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log6_actor
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log6_tgt
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_6 value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_6 value = 0 }
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log7_actor
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log7_tgt
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_7 value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_7 value = 0 }
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log8_actor
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log8_tgt
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_8 value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_8 value = 0 }
		}
	}
	event_target:dtp_log7_actor = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log8_actor
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_8 value = dtp_logcode_7 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_8 value = dtp_logres_7 }
	}
	event_target:dtp_log7_tgt = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log8_tgt
	}
	event_target:dtp_log6_actor = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log7_actor
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_7 value = dtp_logcode_6 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_7 value = dtp_logres_6 }
	}
	event_target:dtp_log6_tgt = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log7_tgt
	}
	event_target:dtp_log5_actor = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log6_actor
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_6 value = dtp_logcode_5 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_6 value = dtp_logres_5 }
	}
	event_target:dtp_log5_tgt = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log6_tgt
	}
	event_target:dtp_log4_actor = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log5_actor
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_5 value = dtp_logcode_4 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_5 value = dtp_logres_4 }
	}
	event_target:dtp_log4_tgt = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log5_tgt
	}
	event_target:dtp_log3_actor = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log4_actor
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_4 value = dtp_logcode_3 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_4 value = dtp_logres_3 }
	}
	event_target:dtp_log3_tgt = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log4_tgt
	}
	event_target:dtp_log2_actor = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log3_actor
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_3 value = dtp_logcode_2 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_3 value = dtp_logres_2 }
	}
	event_target:dtp_log2_tgt = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log3_tgt
	}
	event_target:dtp_log1_actor = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_actor
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_2 value = dtp_logcode_1 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_2 value = dtp_logres_1 }
	}
	event_target:dtp_log1_tgt = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_tgt
	}
	from = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_actor
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_1 value = $CODE$ }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_1 value = $RES$ }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_last_code value = $CODE$ }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_last_ok value = $RES$ }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_t
		}
		event_target:dtp_t = {
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
		}
	}
	else = {
		from = {
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
		}
	}
	log = "DTP | code $CODE$ | result $RES$"
}

dtp_result = {
	hidden_effect = {
		dtp_log = { CODE = $CODE$ RES = 1 }
	}
}

dtp_deny = {
	hidden_effect = {
		dtp_log = { CODE = $CODE$ RES = 0 }
	}
}

dtp_fail = {
	hidden_effect = {
		dtp_log = { CODE = $CODE$ RES = 2 }
	}
}

dtp_auto_host = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = dtp_host_set }
		}
		remove_global_flag = dtp_auto_pick
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
				is_country_type = default
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_global_flag = dtp_auto_pick }
				}
				set_global_flag = dtp_auto_pick
				dtp_make_host = yes
				log = "DTP HOST: auto -> first player country"
			}
		}
		remove_global_flag = dtp_auto_pick
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = dtp_host_set
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
				}
				country_event = { id = dtp.18 }
			}
		}
	}
}

dtp_disarm = {
	from = {
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_cr_pre
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_cr_unb
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_cr_con
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_cr_cet
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_cr_khan
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_gm_kill
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_m_tr
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_m_free
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_pl_tr
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_colony_rm
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_fl_tr
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_fl_del
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_mass_fl
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_subj
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_fed
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_war
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_m_free2
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_bd_clrb
		remove_country_flag = dtp_arm_bd_clrd
	}
}

dtp_res_do = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_res value = 1 }
			}
		}
		dtp_res_op_$OP$ = { RES = energy }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_res value = 2 }
			}
		}
		dtp_res_op_$OP$ = { RES = minerals }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_res value = 3 }
			}
		}
		dtp_res_op_$OP$ = { RES = food }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_res value = 4 }
			}
		}
		dtp_res_op_$OP$ = { RES = alloys }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_res value = 5 }
			}
		}
		dtp_res_op_$OP$ = { RES = consumer_goods }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_res value = 6 }
			}
		}
		dtp_res_op_$OP$ = { RES = influence }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_res value = 7 }
			}
		}
		dtp_res_op_$OP$ = { RES = unity }
	}
	else_if = {

			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_res value = 8 }
			}

	else_if = {

			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_res value = 9 }
			}

	else_if = {

			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_res value = 10 }

		dtp_res_op_$OP$ = { RES = engineering_research }

	else_if = {

	else_if = {

	else_if = {

			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_res value = 14 }
			}
		}
		dtp_res_op_$OP$ = { RES = sr_dark_matter }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_res value = 15 }
			}

		dtp_res_op_$OP$ = { RES = sr_living_metal }

	else_if = {

dtp_res_op_add = {
	event_target:dtp_t = {
		set_variable = { which = dtp_amt value = from.dtp_amount }
		add_resource = { $RES$ = 1 mult = dtp_amt }
	}
}

dtp_res_op_sub = {
	event_target:dtp_t = {
		set_variable = { which = dtp_amt value = from.dtp_amount }
		export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = $RES$ variable = dtp_stock }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_amt value > dtp_stock }
			}
			set_variable = { which = dtp_amt value = dtp_stock }
		}
		multiply_variable = { which = dtp_amt value = -1 }
		add_resource = { $RES$ = 1 mult = dtp_amt }
	}
}

dtp_res_op_set = {
	event_target:dtp_t = {
		set_variable = { which = dtp_amt value = from.dtp_amount }
		export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = $RES$ variable = dtp_stock }
		subtract_variable = { which = dtp_amt value = dtp_stock }
		add_resource = { $RES$ = 1 mult = dtp_amt }
	}
}

dtp_clist = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_cl_1
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_cl_1
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_cl_2
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_cl_2
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_cl_3
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_cl_3
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_cl_4
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_cl_4
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_cl_5
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_cl_5
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_cl_6
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_cl_6
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_cl_7
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_cl_7
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_cl_8
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_cl_8
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_cl_9
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_cl_9
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:dtp_cl_10
		}
		clear_global_event_target = dtp_cl_10
	}
	from = {
		set_variable = { which = dtp_cn value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_coff value = dtp_cpage }
		multiply_variable = { which = dtp_coff value = 10 }
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			dtp_listed_country = yes
		}
		from = {
			change_variable = { which = dtp_cn value = 1 }
			set_variable = { which = dtp_crel value = dtp_cn }
			subtract_variable = { which = dtp_crel value = dtp_coff }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_crel value = 1 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_cl_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_crel value = 2 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_cl_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_crel value = 3 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_cl_3
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_crel value = 4 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_cl_4
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_crel value = 5 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_cl_5
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_crel value = 6 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_cl_6
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_crel value = 7 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_cl_7
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_crel value = 8 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_cl_8
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_crel value = 9 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_cl_9
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_crel value = 10 }
				}
			}
			save_global_event_target_as = dtp_cl_10
		}
	}
	from = {
		set_variable = { which = dtp_cmore value = dtp_cn }
		subtract_variable = { which = dtp_cmore value = dtp_coff }
		subtract_variable = { which = dtp_cmore value = 10 }
	}
}

dtp_gm_on = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = dtp_gm_role }
		}
		set_country_flag = dtp_gm_role
		set_country_type = dtp_gamemaster
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_ethic = ethic_gestalt_consciousness }
			}
			country_add_ethic = ethic_dtp_gamemaster
		}
		add_modifier = { modifier = dtp_gm_role days = -1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_galactic_community_member = yes
			}
			remove_from_galactic_community = yes
		}
		every_owned_planet = {
			limit = {
				has_branch_office = yes
			}
			close_branch_office = yes
		}
		every_system = {
			prev = {
				set_visited = prev
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				dtp_listed_country = yes
				NOT = { is_same_value = prev }
			}
			establish_communications_no_message = prev
			set_closed_borders = { who = prev status = no }
			prev = {
				set_closed_borders = { who = prev status = no }
			}
		}
		log = "DTP GM ROLE: on"
	}
}

dtp_gm_off = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = dtp_gm_role
		}
		remove_country_flag = dtp_gm_role
		set_country_type = default
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ethic = ethic_dtp_gamemaster
			}
			country_remove_ethic = ethic_dtp_gamemaster
		}
		remove_modifier = dtp_gm_role
		log = "DTP GM ROLE: off"
	}
}

dtp_make_host = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = dtp_host
		}
		remove_country_flag = dtp_host
		dtp_gm_off = yes
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = dtp_host_seat
		}
		remove_country_flag = dtp_host_seat
	}
	set_country_flag = dtp_host_seat
	set_country_flag = dtp_host
	set_global_flag = dtp_host_set
	save_global_event_target_as = dtp_host
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = no
			NOT = { has_country_flag = dtp_gm_role }
		}
		country_event = { id = dtp.19 }
	}
}

dtp_close_vote = {
	remove_global_flag = dtp_vote_open
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = dtp_vote_pending
		}
		remove_country_flag = dtp_vote_pending
	}
}

dtp_fire_catalog = {
	from = {
		save_global_event_target_as = dtp_last_actor
	}
	set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 1 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_t
		}
		event_target:dtp_t = { country_event = { id = dtp.1001 } }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 2 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_t
		}
		event_target:dtp_t = { country_event = { id = dtp.1002 } }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 3 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_t
		}
		event_target:dtp_t = { country_event = { id = dtp.1003 } }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 4 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_p = { exists = owner }
		}
		event_target:dtp_p = { planet_event = { id = dtp.1004 } }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 5 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_f
		}
		event_target:dtp_f = { fleet_event = { id = dtp.1005 } }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 6 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_t
		}
		event_target:dtp_t = { country_event = { id = dtp.1006 } }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 7 }
			}
			exists = event_target:dtp_t
		}
		event_target:dtp_t = { country_event = { id = dtp.1007 } }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 8 }
			}
			always = yes
		}
		every_country = { limit = { is_ai = no } country_event = { id = dtp.1008 } }
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 9 }
			}
			dtp_custom_valid_9 = yes
		}
		dtp_custom_fire_9 = yes
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 10 }
			}
			dtp_custom_valid_10 = yes
		}
		dtp_custom_fire_10 = yes
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 11 }
			}
			dtp_custom_valid_11 = yes
		}
		dtp_custom_fire_11 = yes
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 12 }
			}
			dtp_custom_valid_12 = yes
		}
		dtp_custom_fire_12 = yes
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 13 }
			}
			dtp_custom_valid_13 = yes
		}
		dtp_custom_fire_13 = yes
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 14 }
			}
			dtp_custom_valid_14 = yes
		}
		dtp_custom_fire_14 = yes
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 15 }
			}
			dtp_custom_valid_15 = yes
		}
		dtp_custom_fire_15 = yes
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			from = {
				check_variable = { which = dtp_cat value = 16 }
			}
			dtp_custom_valid_16 = yes
		}
		dtp_custom_fire_16 = yes
		set_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { which = dtp_fired value = 1 }
		}
		dtp_result = { CODE = 13 }
	}
	else = {
		dtp_fail = { CODE = 13 }
	}
}

dtp_host_check = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = dtp_host_set }
			NOT = { any_country = { has_country_flag = dtp_host_seat } }
		}
		dtp_auto_host = yes
	}
}

	if = {
